Abstract: Assessing the maturity of artificial intelligence technologies is essential for investment decisions, project management, and policy monitoring, yet the available readiness frameworks are heterogeneous and difficult to apply automatically: the adaptation of Technology Readiness Levels to AI lacks AI-specific gating criteria, the Machine Learning Technology Readiness Levels presuppose access to internal process artifacts, and AI/data readiness dimension models employ scales that resist direct comparison. This paper makes two contributions. First, we unify these three frameworks into the Unified AI Readiness Level (AIRL), a nine-level ordinal scale built on an environmental evidence ladder and complemented by dimensional caps (covering specification, data existence, data quality, data legality, expert knowledge, and algorithmic maturity) together with a generality-anchoring rule and explicit assignment disciplines, so that a readiness level becomes decidable from a natural-language description of the work alone. Second, we propose RAIL (Readiness Assessment via Independent LLM-experts), a panel-of-experts classifier that operationalizes the scale: one evidence agent and six independent dimension agents, each a large language model with a narrowly scoped mandate, deliver verdicts that a deterministic minimum rule aggregates and a chief expert reviews under asymmetric authority, confirming or lowering the panel's recommendation but never raising it above the caps. The method was tested in the analysis of several research works showing consistency and avoiding overestimation from monolithic LLM classifiers.

</details>


### [244] [Academic League of Artificial Intelligence - An Integrative Perspective of Teaching, Research, and Extension](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13447)
*Alison R. Panisson,Maria Eduarda W. M. Vianna,Italo Firmino da Silva,Heitor Henrique da Silva,Rafaela Fernandes Savaris,Bernardo Pandolfi Costa,Martin Augusto Gagliotti Vigil,Jim Lau,Agenor Hentz,Andréa Sabedra Bordin,Alexandre Leopoldo Gonçalves,Roberto Rodrigues-Filho*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了巴西圣卡塔琳娜联邦大学人工智能学术联盟（LIA）采用的组织框架，该框架以学生为中心、基于项目的方式整合教学、研究和大学扩展三大支柱，通过民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织来培养技术与横向能力，并已被证明是灵活可复制的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 学术联盟已成为促进课外教育和加强大学与社会融合的重要机制，但如何设计一个能有效整合教学、研究和大学扩展三大支柱的组织框架仍缺乏系统性方案。

Method: 采用学生为中心、基于项目的方法，结合民主治理、协作学习和动态项目组织三种核心机制，通过竞赛团队、学习小组、公开讲座、知识库和具有社会影响力的人工智能应用等代表性项目来验证框架的可行性。

Result: 该框架成功在统一组织架构内开展了多样化的教育、科研和扩展活动，促进了领导力培养、科学产出、社区参与和知识保留，证明了其灵活性和可复制性。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为将大学三大支柱整合到工程和计算教育中提供了实用指导，可推广至学术联盟及类似学生组织。

Abstract: Academic leagues have become important mechanisms for promoting extracurricular education and strengthening the integration between universities and society. This paper presents the organizational framework adopted by the Academic League of Artificial Intelligence (LIA) at the Federal University of Santa Catarina (UFSC), designed to integrate teaching, research, and university extension through a student-centered, project-based approach. The framework combines democratic governance, collaborative learning, and dynamic project organization to foster both technical and transversal competencies. The framework is illustrated through representative initiatives, including competition teams, study groups, open lectures, knowledge repositories, and AI-powered applications with social impact. These projects demonstrate how diverse educational, scientific, and extension activities can be developed within a common organizational structure while promoting leadership, scientific production, community engagement, and knowledge preservation. The reported experience indicates that the proposed framework provides a flexible and replicable model for integrating the three university pillars into engineering and computing education, offering practical guidance for academic leagues and similar student organizations.

</details>


### [245] [A Unifying Perspective on Causal World Models: From Observations to Representations to Structure](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13456)
*Avinash Kori,Fabrizio Russo*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文从因果视角研究世界模型（WM），提出了因果世界模型（CWM）的形式化定义，主张有用的世界模型不仅要具备生成能力，还应捕捉实体属性、实体间交互及实体与环境间的交互关系，并与因果表示学习、物体中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型和基于模型的决策等领域的现有工作建立联系，同时讨论了可辨识性条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型被视为智能体超越训练分布进行预测、规划和行动的基础，但现有世界模型主要聚焦于生成能力，缺乏对环境中因果关系（实体属性、实体间交互、实体与环境交互）的显式建模，这限制了其在因果推理和决策支持方面的能力。

Method: 论文提出一种跨抽象层次（从感知观察到概念化环境结构）的因果视角来研究世界模型，给出了Causal World Models（CWMs）的形式化定义，并将其与因果表示学习、物体中心学习、因果发现、结构因果模型及基于模型的决策制定等方向的理论框架进行连接与统一。

Result: 论文将世界模型与可辨识性文献相关联，阐明了世界模型的各个组件在何种条件下可以从数据中恢复，以及恢复的等价性程度，从而在世界模型中建立了支持因果推理和知情决策的表示与结构基础。

Conclusion: 该工作从因果视角为世界模型提供了更全面的理论框架，强调世界模型不应仅是生成式模型，还应具备因果结构表征能力，为未来构建真正能进行因果推理和可靠决策的智能体提供了理论依据和方向指引。

Abstract: World Models (WM) are increasingly seen as a foundation for intelligent agents that can predict, plan, and act beyond their training distribution. In this paper, we study WMs from a causal perspective across multiple levels of abstraction, ranging from perceptual observations to building a conceptual representation of the structure governing the environment dynamics. We argue that useful WMs must go beyond generative capabilities alone: they should also capture entity properties, entity-to-entity interactions, and entity-to-environment interactions that determine and explain the dynamics of a system. We provide a formal definition of Causal WMs (CWMs) grounded in the tasks they are intended to support, connecting world modelling with existing work in causal representation learning, object-centric learning, causal discovery, structural causal models, and model-based decision-making. Finally, we relate CWMs to the literature on identifiability, clarifying when the components of a WM can be recovered from data and up to which equivalence. With this, we ground WMs in representations and structures that support causal reasoning and informed decision-making.

</details>


### [246] [MARC v1: An Open-Source Multi-Agent Framework for Clinical AI Reasoning and Coordination](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13476)
*Saisha Shetty,Satvik Tripathi,Austin Lin,Colin Zhao,Theodore Kim,Don Enwerem,Jacinta Arnold,Shahriar Faghani,Tessa S Cook*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: MARC是一个开源的多智能体临床推理框架，通过确定性多智能体编排替代单一LLM提示词，整合抽取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色智能体，支持YAML配置、CPU部署和自动提示词生成。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有临床推理依赖单一LLM提示词方法，缺乏可解释性、难以定位推理阶段错误，且需要领域专家手动进行提示工程，限制了临床场景的落地应用。

Method: MARC框架将临床推理分解为抽取、推理、答案生成和评估四个角色专用智能体，通过显式上下文传递和可追踪的中间输出实现阶段式错误归因；引入Decomposer模块从自然语言描述自动生成任务专属智能体提示词；支持API和本地CPU部署，全部通过YAML配置化，无需修改代码。

Result: 框架为模型无关、可解释、对无编程经验的临床领域专家友好，已开源发布并支持各类部署场景。

Conclusion: MARC通过确定性多智能体编排和自动提示词生成，为临床推理提供了可解释、可配置、易部署的替代方案，降低了临床专家使用LLM的门槛。

Abstract: We present Multi-Agent Reasoning and Coordination (MARC), an open-source framework that replaces monolithic LLM prompting with deterministic multi-agent orchestration for clinical reasoning. MARC coordinates role-specialized agents for extraction, reasoning, answer generation, and evaluation, with explicit context passing and traceable intermediate outputs, enabling stage-wise failure attribution. We additionally introduce a Decomposer module that generates task-specific agent prompts from a plain-language description, eliminating manual prompt engineering. The framework supports both API-based and local CPU-compatible deployments and is entirely configurable via YAML, without code modifications. MARC is designed to be model-agnostic, interpretable, and accessible to clinical domain experts without programming expertise. The full framework is available at https://github.com/Penn-RAIL/MARC-v1.

</details>


### [247] [AlayaWorld: Interactive Long-Horizon World Modeling - Full Technical Report (v1.1)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13492)
*AlayaWorld Team,Kaipeng Zhang,Chuanhao Li,Yifan Zhan,Yongtao Ge,Yuanyang Yin,Jiaming Tan,Kang He,Liaoyuan Fan,Mingliang Zhai,Ruicong Liu,Xiaojie Xu,Xuangeng Chu,Zhen Li,Zhengyuan Lin,Zhixiang Wang,Zian Meng,Zihui Gao*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文介绍了AlayaWorld的改进版本，核心思想是让条件信号在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能贴近生成内容。通过六项修改（包括流式3D点缓存渲染器、运动感知潜在条件、统一VAE编解码协议等），彻底重构了条件的表示与集成方式，同时保持主干架构和自回归生成方案不变。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 先前的AlayaWorld版本中，条件信号的表示和集成方式与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上不够匹配，导致条件信息的利用效率受限。作者提出了一个简单原则：条件信号应尽可能与生成内容在潜在空间和时间结构上保持一致，据此对条件管线进行全面重构。

Method: 沿用原有主干架构、块级自回归生成方案和训练数据，重点重构条件信号表示与集成。主要改动包括：(1) 将静态帧图像条件替换为运动感知的潜在条件；(2) 将重渲染的空间记忆作为连续序列进行因果编码；(3) 在像素空间对齐时间记忆窗口；(4) 采用硬记忆丢弃（移除记忆token而非置零）；(5) 统一训练和推理阶段的VAE编解码协议；(6) 移除相机AdaLN分支，视角控制完全由重渲染空间条件提供。此外，将基于深度扭曲的空间记忆替换为流式3D点缓存渲染器。

Result: 新版本模型在保持相同主干架构和训练数据的前提下，通过条件表示和集成方式的系统性改进，使条件信号与生成内容在潜在空间和时间结构上达到更紧密的匹配，提升了条件信息的利用效率和生成效果。

Conclusion: AlayaWorld的改进验证了"条件信号应与生成内容在潜在表示和时间结构上尽可能匹配"这一设计原则的有效性，为视频生成模型中的条件建模提供了新的思路和工程实践方案。

Abstract: This report presents an improved version of AlayaWorld. While the backbone architecture, chunk-wise autoregressive generation scheme, and training data remain unchanged from the previous release, we substantially revise how conditioning signals are represented and integrated into the model. The new design is guided by a simple principle: conditioning signals should match the generated content as closely as possible in both latent representation and temporal structure. To this end, we make two major changes. First, we replace the previous depth-warping-based spatial memory with a streaming 3D point-cache renderer. Second, we redesign the conditioning pipeline so that visual conditions are encoded in the same causal-VAE latent space, with temporal statistics consistent with those of the generated video. Concretely, the new version introduces six modifications: (1) replacing static-frame image conditioning with motion-aware latent conditioning; (2) causally encoding re-rendered spatial memory as a continuous sequence; (3) aligning the temporal-memory window in pixel space; (4) adopting hard memory dropout that removes memory tokens rather than zeroing them; (5) unifying the VAE encoding and decoding protocol across training and inference; and (6) removing the camera AdaLN branch, such that viewpoint control is provided entirely through the re-rendered spatial condition.

</details>


### [248] [OmniScientist: An Omni-Modal Omni-Discipline AI Scientist](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13558)
*Bobo Li,Hao Fei,Tianjie Ju,Mong-Li Lee,Wynne Hsu*

Main category: cs.AI

TL;DR: 本文提出OmniScientist，一个端到端的全模态AI科学家系统，能够直接从异构原始证据（图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图表）进行多学科研究，通过感知层和三个自主智能体（构思、实验、撰写）在确定性流水线中协同工作，并内置新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性检查。在36个跨5个学科家族的真实数据案例中，系统全部完成从原始数据到编译手稿的全流程，平均论文得分6.3，相比仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体，在所有7个评估维度上均有提升且赢得85%的对比判断。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有AI科学家系统虽然覆盖了研究流程的各个环节，但通常仅基于文本、代码、标签或预计算摘要进行推理，导致科学发现所依赖的空间、时间、跨通道和程序性关系对智能体不可用。作者认为工作流覆盖本身不足以提供完整证据访问，迫切需要让AI科学家能够直接感知多维度的原始证据。

Method: 提出端到端全模态AI科学家系统OmniScientist，包含感知层和三个自主智能体（构思、实验、撰写），在确定性流水线中运行，使观察结果贯穿研究生命周期影响研究问题、实验决策和最终结论；同时在代码层面执行想法、严谨性和声明检查，强制执行新颖性筛查、统计有效性、执行溯源和数值可追溯性。

Result: 在36个真实数据案例（覆盖5个学科家族、4类科学证据，涉及图像、信号、音频、视频、3D结构、轨迹、表格、公式和图等多模态）中，OmniScientist全部完成从原始数据到编译手稿的完整路径，平均论文得分6.3；与仅接收预计算标量特征的盲变体对比，直接感知在所有7个评估维度上均胜出，且赢得85%的逐对比较判断。

Conclusion: 生命周期全程的感知能力对于基于证据的科学发现至关重要，OmniScientist展示了从原始异构证据直接驱动多学科研究的可行性，为构建广泛能力的AI科学家提供了实用路径。

Abstract: Recent advances in foundation models have enabled AI scientists to automate increasingly complete research workflows, from hypothesis generation and code execution to manuscript preparation. Yet workflow coverage alone does not provide access to the full evidence on which scientific discovery depends. Existing systems typically reason over text, code, labels, or precomputed summaries, leaving scientifically decisive spatial, temporal, cross-channel, and procedural relations unavailable to the agent. We introduce OmniScientist, an end-to-end, omni-modal AI scientist that conducts multidisciplinary research directly from heterogeneous raw evidence. A perception layer and 3 autonomous agents for ideation, experiment, and writeup operate within a deterministic pipeline, allowing observations to shape research questions, experimental decisions, and final claims throughout the research lifecycle. By running idea, rigour, and claim checks in code, the system enforces novelty screening, statistical validity, execution provenance, and numerical traceability. We evaluate OmniScientist on 36 real-data cases spanning 5 discipline families, 4 families of scientific evidence, and modalities including images, signals, audio, video, 3-D structures, trajectories, tables, formulae, and graphs. The system completes the full path from raw data to a compiled manuscript in all 36 cases and achieves a mean overall paper score of 6.3 with the reference reasoning backbone. In paired comparisons against a blind variant that receives only precomputed scalar features, direct perception improves all 7 evaluation dimensions and wins 85% of head-to-head judgments. These results show that lifecycle-wide perception is essential for evidence-grounded scientific discovery and provides a practical path toward broadly capable AI scientists.

</details>


<div id='cs.CR'></div>

# cs.CR [[Back]](#toc)

### [249] [DCBA: Detection of Collaborative Black-Hole Attacks in Connected Dominated Set using Baiting Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12347)
*Lata B T,Venugopal K R*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种轻量级的协作式黑洞攻击检测与隔离技术（LW-CBH），通过在AODV路由协议基础上引入基于定时器的诱饵过程和反向追踪机制，利用MAC层的控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）来检测并隔离恶意节点，并通过NS-2.35仿真验证其在吞吐量、数据包投递率、端到端时延和路由开销方面的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MANET网络因缺乏集中管理和动态拓扑特性而容易遭受各类攻击，其中协作式黑洞攻击会严重降低网络性能和数据传输可靠性，而现有的AODV路由协议在动态拓扑变化下难以有效检测恶意节点，因此需要一种轻量且高效的检测与隔离机制。

Method: 提出LW-CBH方案，增强现有AODV路由协议。具体包括：采用基于定时器的诱饵（baiting）过程吸引恶意节点暴露，通过反向追踪（reverse tracing）机制定位恶意节点，并利用MAC层控制状态消息（R-SEQ和C-SEQ）以及连通支配集（connected dominated set）节点来传递和确认控制信息，从而实现对协作式黑洞节点的检测和隔离。

Result: 使用NS-2.35离散事件仿真工具对方案进行验证，评估指标包括吞吐量、数据包投递率、平均端到端延迟和归一化路由开销。仿真结果表明该方案能够有效检测并隔离协作式黑洞攻击节点，改善网络整体性能。

Conclusion: LW-CBH方案通过在AODV协议中引入诱饵和反向追踪机制，有效解决了协作式黑洞攻击的检测和隔离问题，弥补了现有AODV协议在动态拓扑下检测能力不足的缺陷，为MANET安全路由提供了轻量级的解决方案。

Abstract: Mobile Ad-hoc Network (MANET) is temporary and dynamic network topology, wherein nodes are mobile in nature and distributed randomly in a network area. In MANET, nodes cooperate with each other to operate and forward data through multihop communication between source and destination. MANET is exposed to different types of attacks due to absence of central administration. However, some nodes decline to cooperate, misbehaves and appears to be malicious affecting network functionality and connectivity. Providing security and identifying malicious node has become one of the challenging research topics in MANET. Black-hole attack is considered to be most popular attack that degrades the overall network performance. Black-hole node falsely advertises the shortest path to destination intentionally to disrupt the network communication resulting in packet drop. In collaborative black-hole attacks, multiple black-hole nodes cooperate and launch attacks in order to degrade network reliability. In this article we propose a Lightweight technique to detect and isolate Collaborative Black-Hole attacks (LW-CBH) by enhancing existing AODV routing protocol. In this scheme a timer based baiting process and reverse tracing setup is used to detect malicious node through control status message in MAC layer which are Reply Sequence (R-SEQ) and Code Sequence (C-SEQ) message of connected dominated set of nodes. However existing AODV routing protocol fails to detect malicious node during dynamic topology changing in MANET. Simulation of proposed technique is performed using discrete event simulator tool NS-2.35. The simulation results are evaluated for throughput, packet delivery ratio, average end-to-end delay and normalized routing overhead.

</details>


### [250] [The energetic cost of mitigating AI attacks in cellular networks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12431)
*Adrián Losada,Hao Qiang Luo-Chen,David Segura,Carlos S. Alvarez-Merino,Milan Groshev,Emil J. Khatib,Raquel Barco*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了蜂窝网络中AI/ML模型的安全防御技术所带来的能源消耗问题，指出当前防御策略虽有效但计算开销常被忽视，并系统分析了ML精度、鲁棒性与能效三者之间的权衡挑战。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随着AI/ML在蜂窝网络（如O-RAN的RIC）中的广泛部署，数据驱动的算法面临新的安全漏洞，攻击者可通过篡改数据使ML模型性能下降。虽然已有防御措施，但这些防御技术产生的计算负担和能源成本在当前注重能效的背景下往往被忽略，因此需要对此进行系统性研究。

Method: 本文对防御技术的能耗进行了特征化表征（characterisation），并梳理了ML精度、鲁棒性和能效三者之间构成的"三元组"挑战关系。

Result: 研究揭示了防御技术在提供安全保护的同时会引入额外的计算负荷，进而产生能源开销，并明确了ML精度、鲁棒性与能效之间存在需要平衡的张力关系。

Conclusion: 在部署AI驱动的蜂窝网络安全防御时，不能仅关注防御有效性，还需将能源效率纳入考量，以实现精度、鲁棒性和能效之间的综合最优。

Abstract: The integration of Artificial Intelligence (AI), generally as Machine Learning (ML) algorithms, in all levels and aspects of cellular networks demonstrates the success of data-driven algorithms; for example, the Radio Intelligence Controller (RIC) of the O-RAN paradigm bestows the network with optimised radio resource allocation, load balancing or energy efficiency functions, among others. Nevertheless, this dependency on data opens new security vulnerabilities, as attackers can alter data properties and steer ML models to underperform or degrade. Conversely, the developed mitigation strategies are effective, but they generate a computational load which, in consequence, results in an energy cost generally overlooked, even in the current energy-awareness context. In this work, consumption of a defence technique is characterised, and the challenges raised by the triad of ML accuracy, robustness and energy efficiency are outlined.

</details>


### [251] [Non-Degenerate Risk Certification for Automated Security Decisions: A Decision-Contract Theory with ATT\&CK-Aligned Triage as a Worked Instance](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12444)
*Zhenpeng Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文指出无条件的风险界限可能因"永不行动"的选择器而空洞地为零，并提出决策契约理论加以解决。作者建立了错误守恒定律和标签无关的容量证书，排除全弃权方案，并在LLM入侵检测的告警分诊场景中验证：3个IDS数据集、6个LLM、4个错误率阈值下，90.3%配置满足风险目标，83.4%平均正确自动化率，并解释了所有低效用配置的成因。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者发现现有风险证书存在结构性缺陷：无条件风险界限可通过"永不行动"的选择器人为满足，即风险证书的可满足性并不蕴含实际自动化能力。这一空洞失败在LLM入侵检测告警分诊场景中被暴露，亟需理论框架来区分真实能力与"全弃权"式的伪满足。

Method: 提出决策契约理论，核心包括：(1)错误守恒定律——错误仅在有害自动化、人工延迟和语义掩盖之间重新分配；(2)标签无关单例容量——在不依赖标签的情况下证明结构性无能力，细分为可恢复的阈值错位与风险约束下的无能力；(3)非退化可行动性证书——通过构造排除全弃权解。在ATT&CK对齐的入侵检测告警分诊中，对3个IDS数据集、6个LLM和4个错误率阈值进行实证验证，并用细粒度攻击子类型标签验证粗化迁移恒等式。

Result: 在90.3%的配置中经验性假归因风险达到或低于目标，平均83.4%的正确自动化率。容量诊断能解释所有低效用配置；其细化能区分真正的错位与风险约束下的无能力，并通过展示替代阈值得到确认；训练稳定性重跑未发现确认的结构性无能力实例；真实细粒度攻击子类型标签确认了粗化迁移恒等式，掩蔽质量小但非零。

Conclusion: 风险证书不能脱离决策契约而独立成立——任何缺少"行动证明"的风险界限都可能空洞。决策契约理论提供了统一框架，通过错误守恒与容量证书确保风险界具有实际含义，并在LLM入侵检测中得到了充分验证，证明了该方法能有效区分真正的能力不足与可修复的配置问题。

Abstract: An unconditional risk bound on automated decisions can be satisfied without automating anything, since a selector that never acts drives the bound to zero. We show this is structural: any risk certificate is defined over a decision contract, the inputs a system acts on plus the semantic relation under which an output counts correct, and weakening either hides base-classifier error. We develop a decision-contract theory: an error-conservation law showing error is only reassigned among harmful automation, human deferral, and semantic masking; a label-free singleton capacity certifying structural incapacity, with a risk-feasible refinement separating recoverable threshold misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity; and a non-degenerate actionability certificate excluding all-abstain solutions by construction. We instantiate this on ATT\&CK-aligned alert triage for LLM-based intrusion detection, the setting that exposed the vacuity failure. Across 3 IDS datasets, 6 LLMs, and 4 error-rate thresholds, empirical false-attribution risk stays at or below target in 90.3% of configurations, with 83.4% mean correct automation. The capacity diagnostic explains every low-utility configuration; its refinement separates genuine misalignment from risk-constrained incapacity, confirmed by an exhibited alternative threshold; a training-stability re-run finds no confirmed structural-incapacity instance; and real fine-grained attack-subtype labels confirm the coarsening-transfer identity under a genuine many-to-one map, with small but non-zero masking mass.

</details>


### [252] [SoK: From Generation to Consumption of Privacy Documents in Software Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12511)
*Shidong Pan,Clark LaChance,Zhen Tao,Sepideh Ghanavati*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文是一篇系统化知识综述（SoK），从软件工程视角对2010至2025年间290篇关于隐私文档（如隐私政策、隐私标签等）的研究论文进行了全面的生命周期导向分析，围绕五个研究问题展开，并识别出15个关键研究趋势、21个开放机会及四个更广泛的研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 隐私文档研究在过去数十年间急剧扩展，涵盖传统隐私政策、短通知（如隐私标签）以及界面级透明度机制，导致研究者难以获得关于隐私文档在创建、分析、评估和维护全生命周期中的统一视角。作者希望通过提供系统化的知识综述，为该领域建立共享的研究基础。

Method: 作者采用系统文献综述方法，从2010至2025年间筛选并分析了290篇论文，围绕五个研究问题组织分析：(1)隐私文档的定义与范围；(2)生成方式；(3)分析与信息提取；(4)不一致性与不合规检查；(5)可用性评估与改进。基于分析结果进一步归纳研究趋势、开放机会和未来方向。

Result: 本文识别出15个关键研究趋势和21个开放机会，并进一步提出了四个更广泛的研究方向：AI中心平台带来的新兴挑战、对多样化和最新数据基础的需求、基于LLM的统一策略-代码分析，以及面向终端用户和开发者的双重可用性。

Conclusion: 本文通过提供一种统一的、生命周期导向的隐私文档研究视角，填补了该领域缺乏系统整合的空白，为未来研究提供了共享基础和方向指引，特别强调了AI与LLM技术对隐私文档研究的深远影响。

Abstract: Privacy documents (e.g., privacy policies) are a central mechanism through which digital services disclose data practices and seek user consent. Over the past decades, research on privacy documents has expanded significantly, encompassing not only traditional privacy policies but also short notices (e.g., privacy labels) and interface-level transparency mechanisms. As this research area continues to grow, it has become increasingly difficult to obtain a coherent view of how privacy documents are created, analyzed, evaluated, and maintained across their lifecycle. This SoK provides a unified, lifecycle-oriented view of privacy documents from a software engineering perspective. We systematically review and analyze 290 papers published between 2010 and 2025, organizing them around five research questions that examine how privacy documents are (1) defined and scoped, (2) generated, (3) analyzed and extracted, (4) checked for inconsistencies and noncompliance, and (5) evaluated and improved for usability. Building on our findings, we identify 15 key research trends and 21 open opportunities. We further chart four broader research directions that highlight (i) emerging challenges in AI-centric platforms, (ii) the need for diverse and up-to-date data foundations, (iii) LLM-based unified policy-code analysis, and (iv) dual usability for end-users and developers. We hope this SoK provides a shared foundation for future research on privacy policies and privacy documents.

</details>


### [253] [Tracing Provenance and Detecting Tampering with Complementary LLM Watermarks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12713)
*Xiaoyan Feng,Yanjun Zhang,He Zhang,Leo Yu Zhang,Shirui Pan*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种联合提供溯源与篡改证据的大语言模型文本水印方法，通过在每个生成token中共同嵌入鲁棒信号和脆弱信号，实现"完好、篡改、无水印"三态检测，在保持高归属鲁棒性的同时达到最优的篡改检测率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的LLM水印虽然在编辑下能保留溯源能力，但同一鲁棒性使攻击者可以修改关键内容却仍保留归属，这种漏洞被称为piggyback spoofing（搭便车欺骗）。因此需要一种既能追踪来源又能提供篡改证据的水印方案。

Method: 该方法将鲁棒信号和脆弱信号共同嵌入每个生成的token中，两种信号共享相同机制但使用独立密钥和不同种子窗口，使其分别对编辑保持鲁棒性和对读者可见的修改敏感。采用多轮无偏锦标赛重加权保留期望生成分布，通过周期性轮次分配模式控制两种信号的权衡。检测时两信号得分构成二维空间，支持三种决策：完好、篡改和无水印。

Result: 在两个大语言模型和两个提示数据集上的实验表明，该方法在评估方法中实现了最高的篡改检测率，同时保持了具有竞争力的归属鲁棒性和困惑度。消融研究证实可靠的三态检测需要明确定义的完好性概念、两种信号的共同嵌入以及对编辑的互补敏感性。

Conclusion: 该方法成功解决了现有水印在溯源与篡改证据之间的悖论，通过鲁棒与脆弱信号的联合嵌入，在保持溯源能力的同时实现了有效的篡改检测，为LLM生成文本提供了更全面的安全保障。

Abstract: Watermarking LLM-generated text is an important task for tracing its provenance. Existing LLM watermarks preserve provenance under editing, but this same robustness allows an adversary to alter critical content while retaining attribution, a vulnerability known as piggyback spoofing. We introduce an innovative watermark that jointly provides provenance and tamper evidence. It co-embeds a robust signal and a fragile signal into each generated token. The signals share the same mechanism but use independent keys and different seeding windows over normalized text, making one resilient to edits and the other sensitive to reader-visible changes. Multiple rounds of unbiased tournament reweighting preserve the expected generation distribution, while a periodic round-allocation pattern controls the trade-off between the two signals. At detection, their scores form a two-dimensional space supporting three decisions: Intact, Tampered, and No-Watermark. Across two large language models and two prompt datasets, our method demonstrates the highest tamper-detection rate among the evaluated methods while maintaining competitive attribution robustness and perplexity. Ablation studies show that reliable three-state detection requires a well-defined notion of intactness, co-embedding of the two signals, and complementary sensitivity to edits.

</details>


### [254] [PIPES: Securing Agent Perception with Provenance and Priors](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12789)
*Sanjay Kariyappa,Severin Klingler,G. Edward Suh*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出PIPES防御框架，通过语义先验和来源溯源筛选工具使用型代理的响应单元，将状态破坏攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持良性效用（92.5% vs 90.6%）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 工具使用型代理会消费来自不同信任级别来源的外部数据，但工具响应很少标明每个组件的生产者和用途，这种信息缺口使攻击者能够注入内容来伪造代理感知的环境状态，从而让恶意行为看似合理并能绕过现有防护机制，构成状态破坏攻击。

Method: 提出PIPES（Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening）框架，利用语义先验和来源溯源对响应单元进行筛选。当模式提供稳定预期时使用静态字段契约；对开放式内容则基于响应前轨迹和可信的来源元数据条件化筛选。系统标记违反语义先验或溯源层级关系的单元，部署时可选择移除、警告、阻止或升级处理，并在自适应PAIR风格攻击下实例化原子移除进行评估。

Result: 在三个VitaBench和三个AgentDyn数据集划分中，以Gemma 4 31B IT为目标代理，PIPES将平均攻击成功率从84.7%降至2.3%，同时保持良性效用（有PIPES时92.5%，无防御时90.6%）。

Conclusion: PIPES通过结合语义先验与来源溯源信息，能有效防御工具使用代理的状态破坏攻击，且对良性任务性能几乎没有负面影响，验证了溯源感知筛选在代理安全中的可行性和有效性。

Abstract: Tool-using agents consume external data from sources with different levels of trust, yet tool responses rarely identify who produced each component or what it should convey. We show that this gap enables state-corruption attacks, in which attacker-controlled content makes environmental claims beyond the informational authority of its response component and corrupts the agent's perceived environment, making the resulting action appear justified to existing guardrails. We introduce PIPES (Provenance-Informed, Prior-Enforced Screening), which screens response units using semantic priors and source provenance. PIPES uses static field contracts when schemas provide stable expectations, and conditions screening of open-ended content on the pre-response trajectory and trusted provenance metadata. It marks units that violate their semantic prior or the provenance hierarchy; deployments may remove, warn, block, or escalate detected violations. We instantiate atomic removal and evaluate PIPES against adaptive PAIR-style attacks. Across the three VitaBench and three AgentDyn splits with Gemma 4 31B IT as the target agent, PIPES reduces average attack success from 84.7% to 2.3%, while preserving average benign utility (92.5% with PIPES versus 90.6% without defense).

</details>


### [255] [RealmEye: Virtual Machine Introspection for Arm CCA Realm VMs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12822)
*Ruofei Qu,Wei Feng,Hongzhan Ma,Menghan Jia,Muyan Shen,Yu Qin*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了RealmEye，首个面向Arm CCA Realm虚拟机的虚拟机自省（VMI）系统。RealmEye将自省逻辑完全置于Realm Management Monitor（RMM）的R-EL2层，实现硬件强制隔离，无需在Realm内运行任何代理，支持内存/寄存器读取、快照暂停、页级访问陷阱和周期性自驱动扫描，并通过硬件认证通道将结果返回给远程所有者，兼容LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机密虚拟机（CVM）的硬件隔离在防御恶意云的同时，也使得租户无法检测运行在虚拟机内部的内核rootkit（如进程隐藏、内核数据篡改、模型权重窃取等）。现有CVM-VMI系统00SEVen依赖AMD SEV-SNP的VMPL0机制，该机制在Arm CCA平台上不存在，导致Realm虚拟机没有任何自省解决方案。

Method: RealmEye将完整的自省逻辑部署在RMM的R-EL2层：1）直接读取Realm内存和寄存器；2）暂停虚拟机获取一致快照；3）陷阱页级访问；4）通过周期性的自驱动触发模式将扫描时序内置于RMM，防止Hypervisor与rootkit合谋；5）通过硬件认证通道向远程所有者返回结果；6）提供CCA驱动后端使LibVMI和DRAKVUF等现有工具无需修改即可互操作。

Result: 在Arm FVP（Fixed Virtual Platform）上，RealmEye成功检测到Diamorphine rootkit的进程隐藏和syscall表挂钩行为，其RMM内部成本可由原始调用计数线性预测。

Conclusion: RealmEye填补了Arm CCA Realm虚拟机无自省解决方案的空白，通过将自省逻辑置于RMM中实现无需代理、无需修改Realm的硬件级隔离自省，且与现有生态工具兼容，为CCA隐私保护AI推理等敏感云工作负载提供了运行时代码完整性和内核安全监控能力。

Abstract: Confidential VMs (CVMs) have become the dominant substrate for sensitive cloud workloads, from financial services to privacy-preserving AI inference. The hardware isolation that protects these CVMs from a malicious cloud also blinds their owners to what runs inside them: kernel rootkits planted via network or supply-chain attacks can hide processes, tamper with kernel data, and exfiltrate model weights under the cover of the same isolation that defends the VM. Tenants therefore need to inspect a running CVM from outside, yet classical VM introspection (VMI) presupposes a trusted Hypervisor, which CVMs exclude from the TCB. The state-of-the-art CVM-VMI system, 00SEVen, restores introspection on AMD SEV-SNP via an in-VM agent at a privileged tier (VMPL0), a mechanism that does not exist on Arm CCA, leaving Realm VMs without any introspection solution.
  We present RealmEye, the first VMI system for Arm CCA Realm VMs. RealmEye places the entire introspection logic inside the Realm Management Monitor (RMM) at R-EL2, achieving hardware-enforced separation between the monitor and the monitored VM: no agent runs inside the Realm, and the Realm remains unmodified. RealmEye reads Realm memory and registers, suspends the VM for consistent snapshots, and traps page-level accesses, without relying on any in-VM interface. A periodic, self-driven trigger mode keeps scan timing internal to the RMM, preventing the Hypervisor from colluding with in-Realm rootkits. Results are returned to the remote owner over a hardware-attested channel, and a CCA driver backend lets existing tools such as LibVMI and DRAKVUF interoperate with RealmEye unchanged. On the Arm FVP, RealmEye detects process hiding and syscall-table hooking by Diamorphine, and its in-RMM cost is linearly predictable from primitive invocation counts.

</details>


### [256] [Beyond Source: An Empirical Study of Python Bytecode Security Risks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12853)
*Baihong Chen,Tian Xie,Wen Li*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对Python字节码（.pyc文件、编译模块和序列化代码对象）作为安全工件进行了大规模实证研究，分析了超过100万个PyPI发布物，发现7388个包含字节码的工件，并揭示了字节码分析工具的不健壮性、CPython运行时在对抗性字节码下的漏洞以及字节码行为与源码行为的偏差。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Python安全研究主要聚焦于源代码层面，但Python运行时可以直接执行字节码（.pyc文件、编译模块、marshalled代码对象），这造成了检查与执行之间的鸿沟——安全扫描工具看到的源码与运行时实际执行的内容可能不一致，这一安全盲区需要被系统性地量化和研究。

Method: 作者在PyPI上收集了1,034,843个发布工件进行字节码暴露测量；使用版本感知的反编译工具评估字节码的可分析性（覆盖CPython 3.8-3.14）；通过注入对抗性变异字节码测试多个反编译器的健壮性（记录异常、超时和原生进程崩溃）；对CPython运行时进行模糊测试以发现运行时漏洞；最后验证字节码发现能否从源码层面复现。

Result: 在103万+PyPI工件中发现7388个含字节码的工件（含228,578个.pyc文件和28,193个无源码.pyc文件）。对Compython 3.8-3.14的204,904个在范围内文件中，至少有1个反编译器能为204,901个产出源码。工具不健壮：PyPI实际字节码触发托管代码异常和超时，对抗性变异字节码导致反编译器原生进程崩溃，共产生17种健壮性特征。模糊测试产生1009个运行时发现，其中261组具有潜在内存破坏特征，91.7%以上可达文档标注的不安全摄入边界之后的执行，且均无法从普通Python源码复现。

Conclusion: 字节码是生态系统中可见且普遍存在的工件，是实际可行的分析目标，也是具有安全意义的解释器输入——其行为不必与源码层面的行为一致。这要求安全工具链必须将字节码作为一等公民纳入扫描和分析范围，而不能仅依赖源代码层面的检查。

Abstract: Python package security is largely source-centric, yet Python runtimes can execute bytecode directly through .pyc files, compiled-only modules, and marshalled code objects, creating an inspection-execution gap. We present an empirical study of Python bytecode as a security artifact. We measure bytecode exposure in PyPI distributions, evaluate practical analyzability using version-aware tooling, assess CPython runtime robustness under adversarial bytecode, and test source-level reproduction of bytecode findings. Across 1,034,843 collected PyPI artifacts, we identify 7,388 bytecode-containing artifacts, including 228,578 .pyc files and 28,193 artifact-local source-less .pyc files. For modern CPython 3.8-3.14 bytecode, at least one selected decompiler emits source for 204,901 of 204,904 in-scope files, a result measuring emission rather than verified functional equivalence. Tools are non-robust: observed PyPI bytecode triggers managed-code exceptions and timeouts, while adversarial mutated bytecode also drives decompilers into native process failures; together these outcomes yield 17 distinct robustness signatures. Fuzzing produces 1,009 stack-deduplicated runtime findings dominated by pointer-dereference symptoms; 261 groups exhibit potential memory-corruption characteristics, and at least 91.7% of groups reach execution beyond the documented-unsafe ingestion boundary. None reproduce from ordinary Python source. Bytecode is thus a visible ecosystem artifact, a practical analysis target, and a security-relevant interpreter input whose behavior need not match source-level behavior.

</details>


### [257] [Discovering Persistent Behavioural Patterns for Interpretable Blockchain Forensics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12864)
*Dorottya Zelenyanszki,Zhe Hou,Kamanashis Biswas,Vallipuram Muthukkumarasamy*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 提出一个可扩展、与应用无关的框架，用于从大规模区块链活动中发现持久的行为模式。该框架通过行为句构建、两级嵌入（句级和序列级）以及可解释的行为画像器，在超过3000万笔以太坊交易上识别出包括DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器人操作、预言机操纵和rug-pull在内的常规与恶意行为模式，且许多模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有区块链DeFi分析方法多为应用特定、难以扩展或难以解释，缺乏可大规模、通用化且可解释的行为模式发现框架。

Method: 首先构建包含合约、代币和市场上下文的行为句，然后采用两级嵌入流程：句级嵌入捕捉个体行为，序列级嵌入捕捉用户随时间变化的行为；最后通过可解释的行为画像器，从行为主题、常规模式、时间动态、实体暴露和可疑性证据等方面刻画已发现的社区。

Result: 在以太坊超过3000万笔交易上评估，该框架成功揭示常规与恶意两类行为模式，涵盖DEX交易、NFT活动、钓鱼、机器操作、预言机操纵和rug-pull等；多数模式在独立观察窗口间保持稳定，支持长期行为识别。

Conclusion: 该框架兼具可扩展性、可解释性和持久性分析能力，可用于区块链取证调查、行为归因和威胁发现。

Abstract: Public blockchain data enables large-scale DeFi-related analysis, but many existing approaches are application-specific, difficult to scale, or hard to interpret. This research proposes a scalable, application-agnostic framework for \emph{persistent behavioural pattern discovery} from large-scale blockchain activity. It constructs behaviour sentences enriched with contract, token and market context, then applies a two-step embedding process: sentence-level embeddings capture individual actions, while sequence-level embeddings capture user behaviour over time. An interpretable behavioural profiler characterizes discovered communities through behavioural motifs, routines, temporal dynamics, entity exposure, and suspiciousness evidence. Evaluation on Ethereum using over 30 million transactions shows that the framework uncovers both routine and malicious behavioural patterns, including decentralised exchange (DEX) trading, NFT activity, phishing, bot operations, oracle manipulation, and rug-pull schemes. Importantly, many patterns remain stable across independent observation windows, enabling the identification of long-term behaviours beyond a single analysis period. The proposed framework combines scalability, interpretability, and persistence analysis, supporting blockchain forensic investigation, behavioural attribution, and threat discovery.

</details>


### [258] [Labels Are Not Endpoints: Treatment Leakage and Construct Validity in MCP Agent Security Evaluation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12880)
*Rana Muhammad Ahmed,Sabahat Abbas*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文对一次工具使用智能体的安全评估活动进行了事后审计。通过追溯10,200条执行记录至180个模型请求、45个语义请求和15个可观察刺激，发现历史评分器存在直接的处理泄漏——处理元数据门控了ATTACK_SUCCESS类别，导致重新标记处理即可改变固定行为的分类结果。经治疗盲法重建后纠正了58条历史标签，同时保留了三个已验证的受保护数据传输案例和一个独立的未授权转发案例。锁定的v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，双审者盲法一致性审查在96个请求上达成了共识，但在四个构造边界案例上与锁定编码手册存在分歧。作者提出了七环完整性链和可执行的范围受限端点完整性检查器，并明确声明这是战役范围的测量审计，而非人口攻击率、模型排名、防御效能或因果估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全评估经常将存储的标签等同于行为事实，这可能掩盖评估过程中的系统性偏见和泄漏问题。作者旨在通过审计一次保留的活动，揭示评估基础设施中标签与真实行为之间的不一致性，从而为安全测量的可靠性敲响警钟。

Method: 作者对一次保留的安全评估活动进行了三层追溯（10,200条执行行→180个模型请求→45个语义请求→15个可观察刺激）。采用治疗盲法重建评估流程，以排除处理泄漏影响；引入双审者盲法一致性审查协议对96个请求进行独立验证；提出七链完整性框架和可执行的端点完整性检查器以规范化审计过程。

Result: 发现历史评分器存在直接治疗泄漏（处理元数据门控ATTACK_SUCCESS类）；治疗盲法重建纠正了58条错误标签（从ATTACK_SUCCESS或HIJACK_ATTEMPT改为授权良性完成），同时保留三个受保护数据传输案例和一个未授权转发案例；锁定v2普查中ATTACK_SUCCESS记录为零，双审者审查与锁定编码手册在四个构造边界案例上存在分歧。

Conclusion: 该研究是一项战役范围的测量审计，揭示了安全评估中标-行为不对等问题的严重性，但作者明确限定其结论不应用于推断人口攻击率、模型排名、防御效能或因果效应，体现了对研究范围边界的严谨自觉。

Abstract: Security evaluations of tool-using agents often equate stored labels with behavioral facts. We audit a preserved campaign by tracing 10,200 execution rows to 180 model-bound requests, 45 semantic requests, and 15 observable stimuli. Two schema treatments were delivered, but the planned external payload-family corpus was not. The historical grader exhibited direct treatment leakage: treatment metadata gated the ATTACK_SUCCESS class, so fixed behavior could change class under treatment relabeling. A treatment-blind reconstruction corrects 58 historical ATTACK_SUCCESS or HIJACK_ATTEMPT labels to authorized benign completions while preserving three verified protected-data transfers and one separate unauthorized-forwarding case. The locked v2 census contains exactly zero ATTACK_SUCCESS records, while the forwarding case remains a HIJACK_ATTEMPT at a semantic boundary concerning objective completion. A dual-reviewer blinded concordance review of all 96 requests deemed structurally interpretable by locked v2 produced identical reviewer-consensus classes but differed from the locked codebook on four construct-boundary cases. We contribute a seven-link Integrity Chain and an executable, scope-bounded endpoint-integrity linter. The result is a campaign-bounded measurement audit, not a population attack-rate, model-ranking, defense-efficacy, or causal estimate.

</details>


### [259] [Adversarial Robustness in Smishing Detection: A Comparative Analysis of Adversarial Fragility in Classical vs. Transformer-Based Detection Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12889)
*Denzel Chiuseni,Athanase Bahizire,Silva Hama,Jema David Ndibwile*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究了短信钓鱼（smishing）检测系统在低资源场景下的对抗鲁棒性。研究采用27,037条消息数据集，对五种模型架构（三种经典词法模型和两种多语言Transformer）进行三种攻击类型和多种强度的对抗测试，通过鲁棒性退化比率（RDR）衡量性能。结果显示经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下近乎灾难性失败（RDR高达0.988），而Transformer模型表现显著更强（RDR最高0.351）。干净文本性能不能预测对抗鲁棒性，XLM-RoBERTa虽然基线更高但退化更严重。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有短信钓鱼检测系统通常在干净、单语文本上训练和评估，但在低资源场景下，攻击者常通过字符混淆、跨语言代码切换和结构扰动规避系统。作者旨在填补对模型对抗鲁棒性系统评估的空白，检验不同架构在攻击下的真实表现。

Method: 使用27,037条消息的数据集，对五种模型架构（Random Forest、XGBoost、CNN+BiLSTM、mBERT、XLM-RoBERTa）进行评估。经典模型接受黑盒通用攻击，Transformer模型使用注意力引导目标攻击。每种模型在三种攻击类型和多强度水平下测试，使用RDR衡量性能，并用Cliff's d、Mann-Whitney U和Friedman检验进行统计分析。

Result: 经典模型在字符混淆和结构扰动下RDR高达0.988近乎灾难性失败；Transformer模型RDR最高0.351更具韧性，其中结构扰动是其最显著弱点。Transformer组内XLM-RoBERTa基线更高但退化程度大于mBERT。统计分析确认模型架构而非采样导致差异。

Conclusion: 干净文本性能不能作为对抗鲁棒性的可靠预测指标。研究需要架构特定的防御策略，应将短信钓鱼检测视为对抗性网络安全挑战而非静态分类任务。

Abstract: Smishing detection systems are commonly trained and evaluated on clean, monolingual text. In low-resource settings, however, attackers frequently circumvent these systems through character obfuscation, cross-lingual code-switching, and structural perturbation. This study evaluates adversarial robustness for five model architectures: three classical lexical models (Random Forest, XGBoost, CNN+BiLSTM) and two multilingual transformers (mBERT, XLM-RoBERTa), using a dataset of 27,037 messages. Classical models are subjected to black-box generic attacks, while transformers are evaluated with attention-guided targeting. Each model is tested across three attack types and intensity levels, with performance measured by the Robustness Degradation Ratio (RDR). The results reveal a distinct architectural boundary: classical models experience near-catastrophic failure under character obfuscation and structural perturbation (RDR up to 0.988), whereas transformers demonstrate significantly greater resilience (RDR up to 0.351), with structural perturbation representing their most pronounced vulnerability. Effect-size analysis (Cliff's d) indicates a substantial difference between the two model categories. Within the transformer group, XLM-RoBERTa, despite achieving a higher clean-text baseline, exhibits greater degradation than mBERT. These findings demonstrate that clean-text performance is not a reliable predictor of adversarial robustness. Statistical validation using Mann-Whitney U and Friedman tests confirms that these patterns are attributable to model architecture rather than sampling. The results underscore the necessity for architecture-specific defences and frame smishing detection as an adversarial cybersecurity challenge rather than a static classification task.

</details>


### [260] [InterSAGE: The Secure and Verifiable Interoperability Protocol for An Internet of Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13030)
*Zhenhua Zou,Sheng Guo,Qiuyang Zhan,Lepeng Zhao,Shuo Li,Zhuotao Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: InterSAGE是一个面向LLM智能体网络的安全协议套件，通过四层架构（持久身份、发现、信任协商、问责）为智能体间通信提供信任基础设施，可补充现有通信协议（如MCP、A2A等）而不替代它们。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有智能体通信协议只定义了消息交换方式，但缺乏智能体身份验证、授权、能力声明和委托后问责的安全机制，导致跨组织边界的智能体协作存在信任缺失问题。

Method: InterSAGE提出四个核心原语：(1) Agent身份卡绑定开发者、代码包、运营者和部署上下文；(2) 基于DID绑定的可验证凭证清单进行能力感知发现；(3) 结合单调能力衰减与双层访问控制的信任协商；(4) 无需共识账本的内核级加密审计追踪，将使用、委托和执行轨迹绑定到智能体身份。

Result: 与50多个现有方案（涵盖智能体协议、去中心化身份、OAuth/OIDC扩展、零信任治理、委托和审计架构）对比，发现现有工作中没有任何架构能以统一的四层信任基底同时实现持久身份、能力感知发现、信任协商和问责。

Conclusion: InterSAGE作为与通信协议并行而非替代的信任原生协议套件，可让通信协议独立演化，同时保持信任语义的明确性、可移植性和可验证性，为安全智能体互操作提供缺失的安全基石。

Abstract: The emerging Internet of Agents enables LLM-powered agents to discover peers, invoke tools, and delegate tasks across organizational boundaries. Existing protocols increasingly define how agents exchange messages, but not how an agent proves its identity, authorization, advertised capabilities, or accountability after delegation. We present InterSAGE, a trust-native protocol suite that supplies this missing security substrate alongside, rather than in place of, communication protocols. InterSAGE comprises four layers: Persistent Identity, Discovery, Trust Negotiation, and Accountability. Its four core primitives are: (1) Agent Identity Cards that bind developer, code package, operator, and deployment context; (2) capability-aware discovery using DID-bound Verifiable Credential manifests; (3) trust negotiation combining monotonic capability attenuation with two-tier access control; and (4) kernel-mediated cryptographic audit trails that bind usage, delegation, and execution traces to agent identity without a consensus ledger. InterSAGE is designed to complement MCP, A2A, ANP, and AG-UI, allowing communication protocols to evolve independently while keeping trust semantics explicit, portable, and verifiable. We compare InterSAGE with more than 50 efforts spanning agent protocols, decentralized identity, OAuth/OIDC extensions, zero-trust governance, delegation, and audit architectures. We show that no prior architecture jointly enforces persistent identity, capability-aware discovery, trust negotiation, and accountability as a unified four-layer trust substrate for secure agent interoperability.

</details>


### [261] [Beyond Handcrafted Security: Towards Self-Evolving Defense for LLM Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12977)
*Jiajun Ruan,Peiyang Li,Yukun Chen,Fengting Li,Chao Feng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了HARD框架，一种基于harness的自进化运行时防御框架，将LLM代理的运行时防御开发从人工工程设计转变为自主进化过程，自动识别干预策略并基于失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件，在提升安全性能的同时保持良性任务效用。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: LLM代理扩展的操作能力引入了复杂的安全威胁，现有运行时防御严重依赖人工设计的干预措施，缺乏系统化的构建和维护框架。

Method: 首先建立harness级别的运行时防御形式化描述，系统刻画harness机制如何支持防御构建，并从harness视角统一现有运行时防御干预；在此基础上提出HARD框架，自动识别适当的干预策略，并基于观察到的失败轨迹迭代改进防御工件。

Result: 大量实验表明，HARD相比现有手工设计的防御在安全性能上有所提升，同时保持了良性任务的效用。

Conclusion: 自主防御进化是保护已部署LLM代理的有前景的新范式，使代理能够识别防御弱点并持续改进其保护机制。

Abstract: The expanding operational capabilities of large language model (LLM) agents introduce sophisticated security threats. Runtime defenses have emerged as an effective approach to mitigating these risks by integrating security mechanisms into the agent execution loop. However, existing runtime defenses rely heavily on manually designed interventions and lack a principled framework for their construction and maintenance. In this work, we first develop a harness-level formulation of runtime defense that systematically characterizes how harness mechanisms enable defense construction and provides a unified view of existing runtime defense interventions from a harness perspective. Building on this formulation, we propose HARD (Harness-based Autonomous Runtime Defense Evolution), a self-evolving runtime defense framework that automatically identifies appropriate intervention strategies and iteratively improves defense artifacts based on observed failure traces. HARD transforms runtime defense development from manual engineering into an autonomous evolution process, and extensive experiments demonstrate that it improves security performance over existing handcrafted defenses while preserving benign task utility. Our findings highlight autonomous defense evolution as a promising new paradigm for securing deployed LLM agents, enabling agents to identify defense weaknesses and continuously improve their protection mechanisms.

</details>


### [262] [ATOBench: Tracing How Autonomous Penetration-Testing Agents Verify Vulnerabilities When Target Evidence Lies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12996)
*Qiyang Chen,Yixi Li,Fengwei Zhang,Junlin Liu*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出ATOBench，一个可观测自主渗透测试代理验证过程的评估框架。通过注入注册的响应变换，将受欺骗的回合与原生回合配对，并从首个受影响响应开始进行源链接重建，追踪后续动作、证据恢复、停止决策和报告支持。在450个回合上评估五种模型路线，发现增加的活动可能掩盖断裂的验证链，成功恢复取决于找到可用证据并在报告中保留它。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主渗透测试代理依赖目标响应来指导后续动作和最终报告，但欺骗性响应可以重定向攻击轨迹和验证过程。然而，最终报告无法揭示代理如何解释冲突证据、改变路线、决定停止或将观察转化为漏洞声明。需要一种使验证过程可观测的评估框架。

Method: ATOBench在运行时注入注册的响应变换，并将每个变换后的回合与同一环境下的原生回合配对。每对在首个受影响的响应处对齐。采用源链接重建追踪后续动作、证据恢复、停止和报告支持。定义了三种冻结的观察契约：利用证明、资源所有权和可复用工件。在450个回合上评估五种模型路线。

Result: 分析表明，增加的活动可能掩盖断裂的验证链，而成功的恢复取决于找到可用证据并在报告中保留它。五种模型路线的对比揭示了不同模型在证据处理和验证链维护方面的差异。

Conclusion: ATOBench将欺骗性目标观测转化为可复现的证据处理探针，提供了超越最终结果的流程级视角，揭示不可信观测如何影响自主渗透测试中的动作、验证和报告。

Abstract: Autonomous penetration-testing agents rely on target responses. These responses guide both subsequent actions and the final report. A deceptive response can therefore redirect both the attack trajectory and the agent's verification process. However, final reports reveal little about how an agent interprets conflicting evidence, changes course, decides to stop, or turns observations into a vulnerability claim. We introduce ATOBench, an evaluation framework that makes this verification process observable. ATOBench injects registered response transformations at runtime and pairs each transformed episode with a native episode under the same environment. Each pair is aligned at the first affected response. A source-linked reconstruction then follows later actions, evidence recovery, stopping, and report support. Three frozen observation contracts cover exploit proof, resource ownership, and reusable artifacts. We evaluate five model routes over 450 episodes. The analysis shows that increased activity can mask a broken verification chain, while successful recovery depends on finding usable evidence and preserving it through reporting. ATOBench turns deceptive target observations into a reproducible probe of evidence handling in autonomous penetration testing. This process-level view extends offensive pentest agent evaluation beyond final outcomes by revealing how untrusted observations shape actions, verification, and reporting.

</details>


### [263] [OmniSphinx: Active Mix Networks (Extended Version)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13008)
*Daniel Schadt,Christoph Coijanovic,Shabi Shabani,Thorsten Strufe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型混合网络数据包格式OmniSphinx，它通过在数据包中嵌入处理代码，使发送方能够自行决定数据包的处理方式，从而在单一部署中模拟任何其他混合网络格式，解决了现有格式不兼容且需要单独基础设施的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有混合网络数据包格式各有特定目标且相互不兼容，需要部署单独的软件和基础设施，这增加了匿名通信系统的复杂性和部署成本。

Method: 提出OmniSphinx格式，核心创新在于发送方在其数据包中嵌入代码以决定数据包的处理方式，构建了一个能够模拟其他混合网络格式的主动式混合网络。

Result: 实证评估显示，OmniSphinx的模拟开销在典型混合网络用例中是合理的：对于最紧凑的Sphinx格式，计算时间增加约90微秒，数据包头大小增加33%。

Conclusion: OmniSphinx能够在单一部署环境中模拟多种混合网络格式，为匿名通信提供了统一的、灵活的解决方案，同时保持了可接受的性能开销。

Abstract: Mix networks are an important tool to implement anonymous communication, which protects not just the content but also the metadata of messages. Over time, various packet formats for mix networks have been proposed, usually with single, specific goals in mind. These formats are incompatible with each other, requiring separate software and infrastructure to be set up. In this paper, we propose a new format, OmniSphinx, which solves this issue. In OmniSphinx, senders embed code in their packets that determines how they must be processed. The resulting active mix network can emulate any other mix format within a single deployment. Our empirical evaluation shows that emulation in OmniSphinx incurs reasonable overhead compared to native execution for typical mix network use cases: For Sphinx, the most compact format, computation time increases by around 90μs, while headers increase by 33% in size.

</details>


### [264] [InSPECtor: Improving SLEIGH Processor Specification Veracity via Proxy](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13042)
*Michael Chesser,Paul Quirk,Douglas Cooke,Guy Farrelly,Surya Nepal,Damith C. Ranasinghe*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了InSPECtor，一个基于代理自动化Oracle验证策略的测试框架，首次实现对开源SLEIGH处理器规范（Ghidra使用）的系统性验证。通过利用规范自身结构枚举可解码指令形式并生成目标初始状态，将执行处理器规范的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试，在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430等规范中发现38,920处差异，定位125个独特缺陷并提出修复方案，最终提炼出8条改进建议。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 处理器规范支撑着反汇编器、反编译器和模拟器等关键安全与程序分析工具，但其正确性很少被检验。规范中的错误会扭曲程序行为、掩盖漏洞并促成分析规避技术。验证处理器规范是一项挑战性任务，此前缺乏系统性的验证方法。

Method: 设计并实现基于自动化Oracle验证策略（proxy-based）的测试框架InSPECtor。该方法利用规范本身编码的结构来枚举可解码的指令形式并生成有针对性的初始状态，随后通过将执行处理器规范的模拟器与硬件参考进行差分测试，检验指令的成功解码与模拟结果。

Result: 在x86-64、AArch64、ARM/Thumb、RISC-V、MSP430等多样化的开源规范（涵盖不同的规范风格、作者偏好和ISA设计）中，发现超过38,920处差异，归纳出125个带有修复建议的独特缺陷，识别了解码和语义缺陷以及跨厂商不一致性，并提炼出8条具体改进建议。

Conclusion: 研究工作强调了处理器规范正确性的重要性，提供了一个实用的工具来显著提高SLEIGH处理器规范的保真度，从而增强下游安全和分析工具的可靠性。

Abstract: Processor specifications underpin critical security and program- analysis tools such as disassemblers, decompilers, and emulators, yet, their correctness is rarely examined. Errors in specifications distort program behaviour, obscure vulnerabilities, and enable analysis-evasion techniques. Validating processor specifications is a non-trivial task. Our study is a significant undertaking to enable, for the first time, the systematic validation of open-source SLEIGH language specifications, predominantly used by Ghidra. We design and implement a testing framework based on an automated oracle validation strategy by proxy. Our approach leverages the structure encoded in a specification itself to enumerate decodable instruction forms and generate targeted initial states. Then differentially test the successful decoding and emulation of those instructions by comparing emulators exercising the processor specification against hardware references.
  Applying InSPECtor across diverse, open-source specifications---x86-64, AArch64, ARM/Thumb, RISC-V, MSP430---embedding differences in specification styles, author preferences, and instruction set architecture designs, we uncovered over 38,920 discrepancies that led to 125 unique bugs with proposed fixes, identifying decoding and semantic defects as well as cross-vendor inconsistencies. We distill our findings into 8 concrete recommendations to drive future improvements. Our work underscores the importance of specification correctness and provides a practical tool to substantially improve the fidelity of SLEIGH processor specifications, strengthening the reliability of downstream security and analysis tools.

</details>


### [265] [Operationalizing Cyber Threat Intelligence with GraphRAG](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13050)
*Atul Kabra,Prakhar Paliwal,Manjesh K. Hanawal*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文研究将安全研究报告输入知识图谱检索系统（Microsoft GraphRAG）与标准向量相似度检索系统（Naive RAG）生成检测规则的差异。研究发现GraphRAG生成的检测计划更依赖"痛苦金字塔"顶层的持久性线索（如TTPs），而非易变的IOCs（IP、域名、哈希值）。案例研究中，GraphRAG计划的检测在IOC全部轮换后仍保持100%命中率，而Naive RAG计划仅剩29%。在来自四家厂商的九份CTI报告上重复实验确认了这一模式。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 安全分析师从网络攻击报告中生成检测规则时，传统自动化方法只能提取简单的IOC（IP、域名、哈希）并转化为黑名单，但攻击者可在数小时或数天内改变这些线索导致检测失效。作者希望探索是否能用知识图谱检索系统生成更依赖持久性、难以规避的顶层线索（如TTP）的检测计划。

Method: 在同一个报告、相同的生成指令和相同的语言模型条件下，仅改变检索环节：对比Microsoft GraphRAG（知识图谱检索）与Naive RAG（标准向量相似度检索）生成的检测计划。通过案例研究（一份APT28报告）和跨四家厂商的九份真实CTI报告进行重复比较，评估检测计划在IOC轮换后的存活性及其在痛苦金字塔上的层级分布。

Result: 在APT28案例研究中，所有IP、域名和文件哈希被轮换后，GraphRAG计划仍保持100%的检测命中率，而Naive RAG计划仅保留29%。在九份CTI报告（来自四家厂商）的重复实验中确认了相同模式：GraphRAG计划一致达到更高、更难规避的金字塔层级，即使两者的总分有时接近。

Conclusion: 知识图谱感知的检索（GraphRAG）应被视为自动生成SOC可部署狩猎计划的架构正确基础。同时，生成提示词的措辞对结果的影响几乎与检索后端本身一样重要。

Abstract: When a security researcher publishes a report on a cyberattack, detection engineers are supposed to turn it into working detection rules. In practice, most automated attempts at this only extract the simplest clues from the report --- bad IP addresses, domain names, and file hashes --- and turn them into block lists. This is a weak strategy, because attackers can change these simple clues within hours or days, so the resulting detections stop working almost as soon as they are deployed. Security teams describe this idea with the Pyramid of Pain. This project asks whether feeding a report into a knowledge-graph retrieval system, Microsoft GraphRAG, rather than a standard vector-similarity retrieval system (Naive RAG), produces detection plans that rely more on these durable, top-of-pyramid clues. Both systems are given the same report, the same generation instructions, and the same language model to write the final plan; only the retrieval step differs. In a detailed case study of one APT28 report, the GraphRAG plan kept firing at 100\% of its detections after every IP address, domain, and file hash in the report was rotated, while the Naive RAG plan kept firing at only 29\%. Repeating the comparison across nine real CTI reports from four vendors confirms the same pattern: GraphRAG plans consistently reach higher, harder-to-evade levels of the pyramid, even when the two systems end up close on total score. The results support treating knowledge-graph-aware retrieval as the architecturally correct foundation for automatically generating SOC-deployable hunting plans, while showing that the wording of the generation prompt matters almost as much as the retrieval back-end itself.

</details>


### [266] [Smart Contract Invariants Protect Against Cybercriminals](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13191)
*Sofia Bobadilla,Humaira Afrin,Angela Novelli,Martin Monperrus*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出智能合约不变量（program invariant）是对抗区块链网络攻击的最有力解决方案，并构建了包含28个真实以太坊漏洞利用的基准测试集INVARIANTEVAL，结合PONDEREPLAY重放框架验证了不变量能够阻止所有攻击，并通过对108,637笔历史交易的全面重放得到验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 区块链是最具对抗性的计算环境，数十亿美元被网络罪犯通过漏洞窃取，这是一个开放问题，目前尚无任何概念或技术被证明真正有效。

Method: 提出经典的程序不变量概念作为解决方案；设计了原创实验协议，构建INVARIANTEVAL基准（28个真实以太坊漏洞利用，每个配有人类编写的可阻断攻击的不变量）；开发PONDEREPLAY重放框架来验证智能合约不变量的正确性和可靠性。

Result: 智能合约不变量阻断了INVARIANTEVAL中的所有网络犯罪攻击，并通过重放108,637笔历史交易得到全面验证；大规模实验证明不变量能有效保护智能合约免受网络罪犯攻击。

Conclusion: 智能合约不变量是保护区块链免受网络攻击的强大且有效的解决方案，实验证据充分支持该方法的实际防护能力。

Abstract: Blockchains are among the most adversarial environments in computing. Billions are stolen by cybercriminals who exploit vulnerabilities. This is an open problem and no concept or technique has proven to really make a difference. In this paper, we claim that the classical notion of program invariant is perhaps the most powerful solution to the problem. We devise anoriginal experimental protocol to 1) study how invariants would have protected against past real-world attacks and 2) whether state-of-the-art automated tools can find them. The experimental toolchain is sophisticated. It is based on INVARIANTEVAL, a benchmark of 28 real Ethereum exploits, each paired with a human-authored invariant that blocks the attack. We validate every invariant with PONDEREPLAY, a replay framework that re-executes transactions in order to prove the correctness and soundness of smart contract invariants. We demonstrate that smart contract invariants block all the cybercriminal attacks in INVARIANTEVAL, fully validated by replaying 108,637 historical transactions. Our large-scale experiments clearly demonstrate that smart contract invariants protect against cybercriminals.

</details>


### [267] [A Commitment-Based Hybrid Post-Quantum Cryptographic Model for Multi-File Cloud Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13138)
*Lemdi Frank Prikutse,Regina Esi Turkson,Alimatu-Saadia Yussiff,Abdul-Lateef Yussiff,Maame G. Asante-Mensah*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于承诺的混合后量子云存储认证模型，通过批量承诺将后量子签名次数从每文件一次降为每批一次，大幅降低批量上传的认证开销。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 云存储客户端需要抵御未来量子攻击的认证机制，但将混合后量子方案朴素扩展到多文件上传时，每文件一次的后量子格签名成本过高，在现实批量场景下开销不可接受。

Method: 采用AES-256-GCM批量加密、X25519与ML-KEM-768混合密钥封装、Ed25519与ML-DSA-65混合双重签名，基于SHA3-256批量承诺将所有密文绑定为一个固定大小摘要，仅对摘要签名一次，签名调用次数从n降为1，其余操作由快速对称加密处理。

Result: 在普通客户端平台上平均20次实验，签名阶段耗时随批量增长保持近恒定，而逐文件基线呈线性增长。n=1000时，对100KB、1MB、10MB文件分别将签名阶段耗时降低629倍、606倍和725倍。

Conclusion: 批量承诺策略可有效消除后量子签名在批量上传场景中的性能瓶颈，使混合认证方案在大规模文件上传中保持实用性和可扩展性。

Abstract: Cloud storage clients increasingly require authentication that remains secure against future quantum-capable adversaries, motivating hybrid constructions that combine classical primitives with standardized post-quantum alternatives. Extended naively to multi-file upload, such constructions incur a per-file lattice signing cost that dominates authentication time and becomes prohibitive at realistic batch sizes. This paper presents a commitment-based hybrid post-quantum model that addresses this bottleneck. It comprises AES-256-GCM bulk encryption, a hybrid X25519 with ML-KEM-768 key encapsulation mechanism, and a hybrid Ed25519 with ML-DSA-65 dual signature, computed over a SHA3-256 batch commitment. The commitment binds all ciphertexts in a batch to a single fixed-size digest that is signed once, reducing the number of post-quantum signature invocations per batch from n to one, independent of batch size; the remaining encryption and hashing is bounded by fast symmetric throughput. On a commodity client platform, averaged over 20 repetitions, this holds signing-phase time near-constant as the batch grows while the per-file baseline scales linearly. At n = 1000, the model reduces signing-phase time by factors of 629, 606, and 725 for 100 KB, 1 MB, and 10 MB files respectively, against a per-file dual-signing baseline sharing every other primitive.

</details>


### [268] [Slow and Steady: Preventing MEV with Verifiable Delays](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13271)
*Zeta Avarikioti,Dimitris Karakostas,Karl Kreder,Shreekara Shastry*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种针对分布式账本中最大可提取价值（MEV）机会的防御机制，通过强制交易生成时具有可验证延迟，使区块创建者无法在不破坏活性的情况下对MEV机会做出反应。作者在拜占庭模型和理性参与者博弈论模型中均给出正面结果，同时也提出了负面界限来勾勒该防御路线的局限性，并基于历史MEV数据验证了其现实可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: MEV是区块链生态中一个严重威胁，区块创建者可以利用交易排序权提取价值，损害用户利益和协议公平性。作者希望设计一种能够有效防御MEV攻击的机制，在理论上提供可行的方案。

Method: 通过强制实施可验证延迟（verifiable delay）来生成交易，使得区块创建者难以在短时间内对出现的MEV机会作出反应。研究涵盖拜占庭容错模型和博弈论理性参与者模型，并引入负面界限分析来界定该方案的适用边界。

Result: 在拜占庭和博弈论框架下均获得了正面防御结果；同时给出了负面界限，说明该防御路线存在固有局限。基于历史MEV数据的实证分析显示该机制可以实际防范大多数现存的MEV威胁。

Conclusion: 基于可验证延迟的防御机制是应对MEV问题的一个有前景的方向，既有理论支撑也有实践价值，但仍然存在需要进一步研究的边界限制。

Abstract: Our work presents a defense mechanism against Maximal Extractable Value (MEV) opportunities in distributed ledgers. The mechanism relies on the idea of enforcing a verifiable delay when generating transactions, such that a block creator cannot react to the appearance of a MEV opportunity without breaking liveness. We present positive results both in the Byzantine setting and in a game theoretic model of rational participants. We additionally present negative bounds that outline the limitations of this line of defense. Finally, we explore real-world implementation details of verifiable delays and show that, based on historical MEV data, our mechanism could realistically help prevent most existing MEV threats.

</details>


### [269] [VR-Themis: A Scalable Framework for Virtual Reality Application Clone Detection](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13290)
*Gengyang Xu,Hanyang Guo,Hong-Ning Dai,Weizhi Meng*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出VR-Themis，一个基于层次-对象-行为（HOB）的两阶段VR应用克隆检测框架，通过粗粒度聚类和细粒度相似度分析，在4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用且无误报。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的应用克隆检测方法主要针对移动平台（如Android），未能捕捉虚拟现实（VR）应用的独特特征，因此无法有效检测VR市场中的克隆应用，而这类非法行为严重威胁用户安全与开发者版权。

Method: 提出两阶段检测框架VR-Themis：粗粒度阶段基于可检索的统计特征对应用进行聚类，使其可扩展到大规模VR应用数据集；细粒度阶段对粗粒度阶段识别出的可疑应用，使用定义的HOB（层次-对象-行为）度量计算相似度进行深入分析。

Result: 在收集的4,277个VR应用中成功检测出307个疑似克隆应用，且无误报（false positives），验证了该方法的有效性和可扩展性。

Conclusion: VR-Themis利用VR应用的独特特征，通过两阶段的层次化检测策略，能够有效且可扩展地检测VR应用克隆，弥补了现有移动平台检测方法在VR领域的不足。

Abstract: Repackaging of mobile applications (aka app cloning) not only threatens the security and privacy of mobile users but also infringes upon the copyright of the original app developers. However, existing detection methods that primarily focus on mobile platforms (such as Android) fail to capture the essential features of virtual reality (VR). Consequently, they are inadequate for effectively detecting cloned VR apps, which have often been targeted by illegal users in the VR market. Considering the unique features of VR apps, this paper proposes a two-stage app clone detection framework, namely VR-Themis, based on \emph{Hierarchy-Object-Behaviour} (HOB). Firstly, VR-Themis exploits the coarse-grained stage to cluster apps based on their retrievable statistical features, making this tool scalable to large-scale VR app datasets. Then, in the fine-grained stage, VR-Themis performs in-depth analysis of the suspicious apps (identified in the first stage) by calculating similarity using our defined \emph{HOB metrics}. Our extensive experiments indicate that VR-Themis successfully detects 307 suspected clone apps from the collected 4,277 VR apps without false positives, demonstrating its effectiveness and scalability.

</details>


### [270] [TeleGapper: On the (un)reliability of Privacy Policies in Telegram Mini apps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.13390)
*Luca Ferrari,Mariano Ceccato,Luca Verderame*

Main category: cs.CR

TL;DR: 本文提出了TeleGapper，一个黑盒动态分析框架，用于评估Telegram Mini Apps的隐私状况。通过捕获运行时网络流量、识别第三方通信，并与隐私披露信息进行对比，对tApps Center收集的278个Mini Apps进行了评估，发现59.4%的应用会联系至少一个未披露的第三方，78.8%完全依赖Telegram默认隐私政策，且没有任何应用提供同意或退出机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Telegram Mini Apps作为Telegram客户端内嵌的Web应用，形成了庞大的第三方服务生态系统，但其隐私属性鲜有研究。与WeChat等采用严格受控专有执行框架的生态系统不同，Telegram让Mini Apps在WebView中运行，结合平台提供的上下文、标准Web能力和不受限制的出站网络连接，使得敏感信息可能通过普通Web请求被传输给分析、广告、追踪等第三方，且透明度有限。

Method: 作者开发了TeleGapper，这是一个黑盒动态分析框架，核心功能包括：捕获Mini Apps运行时的网络流量、识别第三方通信、以及将观察到的数据流与隐私披露信息进行对比分析，从而评估应用的隐私姿态。

Result: 对从tApps Center收集的278个可正常工作的Mini Apps进行评估后发现：59.4%的Mini Apps联系了至少一个未披露的第三方；78.8%完全依赖Telegram的默认隐私政策；没有任何一个应用提供同意或退出机制。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Telegram Mini Apps生态系统中存在的显著透明度与合规性差距，表明这一广泛使用但研究不足的生态系统在隐私保护方面存在严重不足，需要引起关注和改进。

Abstract: Telegram Mini Apps are Web applications embedded within the Telegram client, forming an ecosystem of third-party services within one of the world's most widely used messaging platforms. Despite their growing adoption and access to Telegram-provided context, their privacy properties remain largely unexplored. Unlike ecosystems such as WeChat, which rely on tightly controlled, proprietary execution frameworks, Telegram adopts a different model: Mini Apps run inside a WebView, combining platform-provided context with standard Web capabilities and unrestricted outbound networking. This enables applications to transmit sensitive information to analytics, advertising, tracking, or other third parties through ordinary Web requests, often with limited visibility.
  Privacy disclosures are therefore critical for transparency. Telegram allows Mini Apps either to define an application-specific privacy policy or to rely on a platform-provided default policy. While the latter reduces the developer's disclosure burden, it may lead to generic statements that do not accurately capture actual data practices of individual Mini Apps.
  In this paper, we present TeleGapper, a black-box dynamic analysis framework to assess the privacy posture of Mini Apps by capturing runtime network traffic, identifying third-party communications, and comparing observed data flows against disclosed privacy information. We evaluate 278 working Mini Apps collected from tApps Center, a community-driven catalogue for discovering third-party applications in Telegram. We find that 59.4% contact at least one undisclosed third party, 78.8% rely exclusively on Telegram's default privacy policy, and none provides a consent or opt-out mechanism. These findings expose a substantial transparency and compliance gap in a widely used yet understudied ecosystem.

</details>


<div id='cs.MA'></div>

# cs.MA [[Back]](#toc)

### [271] [Entropy-Augmented Multi-Objective Policy Optimization in Multiagent Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12534)
*Jamie Santos,Ayhan Alp Aydeniz,Raghav Thakar,Kagan Tumer*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种熵增强策略评估方法，在NSGA-II多目标进化算法的基础上引入行为空间多样性信号，通过熵奖励惩罚行为同质性，从而在自主智能体团队的多目标优化中提升行为多样性，实验表明在漫游车域中相比NSGA-II基线提升了最高48%的超体积指标。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有NSGA-II等多目标进化算法只优化目标空间的多样性，忽略了行为空间的多样性，可能导致过早收敛和行为塌缩，无法在多变外部条件下区分不同策略。

Method: 在智能体适应度评分中引入熵奖励项，结合行为空间多样性信号，同时保留原有的Pareto优化框架，以鼓励种群探索行为上不同的策略。

Result: 在具有不同奖励结构的漫游车域实验中，该方法相对NSGA-II基线在超体积指标上取得了最高48%的提升。

Conclusion: 行为多样性是改善多目标多智能体进化优化的一个有前景且尚未充分探索的方向。

Abstract: Autonomous agent teams deployed in settings such as marine and extraterrestrial outposts must coordinate actions to achieve optimal outcomes across multiple competing objectives. Multi-objective evolutionary algorithms such as NSGA-II optimize for diversity in the objective space, but neglect diversity in the behavior space, possibly leading to premature convergence and a collapse in behaviors that may differentiate policies in different external conditions. To address this, we introduce an entropy-augmented policy evaluation strategy that incorporates an entropy bonus into agent fitness scores, discouraging behavioral homogeneity across the evolving population. By augmenting policy evaluation with a behavior-space diversity signal while preserving the underlying Pareto optimization framework, our method is designed to encourage exploration of behaviorally distinct policies in multiagent domains. We evaluate our approach across rover-domain experiments with qualitatively distinct reward structures and observe hypervolume improvements of up to 48% relative to the NSGA-II baseline, suggesting that behavioral diversity is a promising and underexplored direction for improving multi-objective multiagent evolutionary optimization.

</details>


### [272] [Do LLMs Beat Nash? Testing Decentralized Coordination in Self-Play Multi-Agent Games](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12547)
*Deborah Sinishaw,Qile Zhu,Edwin Meriaux,Gregory Dudek*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文研究了大型语言模型智能体在无法通信的情况下，能否通过推理“对手也是相同模型”来超越标准博弈论的无协调基线。研究发现两个前沿托管模型在双人矩阵游戏中持续超越纳什均衡基准，接近最优联合结果，而多数开源模型仅获得部分收益且高度依赖游戏结构；在四人及以上团队游戏中性能显著下降。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有研究通常假设无中央控制器的LLM智能体需要通信才能协调行动，但本文质疑这一假设，探索在无通信约束下，独立运行的相同模型实例能否仅凭对对手的推理能力超越标准博弈论的无协调基线，从而揭示LLM在多智能体场景中的固有协调能力。

Method: 构建了一个单轮、无通信博弈基准，涵盖13种语言模型，每个模型仅被告知其对手运行的是同一模型。基准包含两种博弈类型：七种原型的两玩家矩阵博弈（每玩家2-10个动作），以及四人及以上的团队博弈，评估指标为相对于底层博弈纳什均衡的表现。

Result: 两个前沿托管模型在双人矩阵博弈中持续超越纳什基线，在多种原型中接近最优联合结果；大多数开源模型仅取得部分收益且随游戏结构变化显著。在四人及以上可互换智能体团队博弈中，所有模型性能大幅下降，尤其是动作空间扩大时更为明显。

Conclusion: 无通信条件下，部分先进LLM具备基于自我推理的超博弈论协调能力，但这种能力局限于双人场景，无法有效推广到更大规模的多智能体团队中，说明驱动双人自博弈收益的能力不具备跨规模迁移性。

Abstract: Large language model agents deployed without a central controller are often assumed to require communication to coordinate their actions. We ask what remains possible without it: when independent instances of the same model cannot communicate, can they still reason about their counterparts well enough to exceed the standard game-theoretic baseline for uncoordinated play? We introduce a benchmark of one-shot, no-communication games in which each of thirteen language models is told only that its counterparts are running the same model and is evaluated against the Nash equilibrium of the underlying game. In two-player matrix games spanning seven archetypes and two to ten actions per player, two frontier-hosted models consistently exceed their Nash benchmark, approaching the optimal joint outcome in several archetypes, while most open-weight models achieve only partial gains that vary sharply by game structure. Performance degrades substantially in team-based games with four or more interchangeable agents, particularly as the action space grows, suggesting that whatever capability drives self-play gains in dyadic games does not transfer to larger multi-agent teams.

</details>


### [273] [Discovering Efficient and Explainable Communication Topologies for LLM-based Multi-Agent Systems via Causal Inference](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12921)
*Junzhi Li,Peng He,Qirui Ji,Wei Wang,Lixiang Liu,Chuxiong Sun*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出了E2-Explainer，一个模型无关的框架，用于为任意拓扑生成器产生的LLM多智能体系统通信拓扑提供可解释的因果归因分析，通过识别关键通信子图来揭示哪些通信边对成功协作至关重要，并可直接用于剪枝以降低通信成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有LLM多智能体系统的拓扑生成方法主要通过黑盒优化学习通信拓扑，仅由任务级奖励驱动，缺乏对为何选择特定通信边的可解释性，难以识别对成功协作起关键作用的通信子图。

Method: 将拓扑解释形式化为因果归因问题，通过Granger风格的优化目标，度量掩蔽每个通信通道对任务结果和最终响应稳定性的影响来获取边级证据，然后将预算受限的子图蒸馏为摊销解释器，实现高效的后验解释。

Result: 在多个推理和编码基准上的广泛实验表明，E2-Explainer能够识别维持成功协作的关键通信子图，这些子图可直接执行以剪枝冗余通信边，在保持竞争性任务性能的同时大幅降低通信成本。

Conclusion: E2-Explainer通过提供可解释的因果归因分析，不仅揭示了多智能体系统中关键通信结构，还实现了通信成本的显著降低，弥补了现有黑盒拓扑优化缺乏可解释性的不足。

Abstract: The performance of large language model (LLM)-based multi-agent systems (MAS) largely depends on effective communication topologies. Existing topology generation methods, however, typically learn communication topologies through black-box optimization driven solely by task-level rewards. While effective, such optimization provides little insight into why particular communication edges are selected, making it difficult to identify the critical communication subgraphs responsible for successful collaboration. To address this limitation, we propose E2-Explainer, a model-agnostic framework for providing interpretable explanations of communication topologies produced by arbitrary topology generators. Specifically, we formulate topology explanation as a causal attribution problem that identifies compact communication subgraphs supported by edge-level evidence of task preservation. We obtain this evidence with a Granger-style objective that measures how masking each communication channel changes the task outcome and the stability of the final response. The resulting budgeted subgraphs are then distilled into an amortized explainer, enabling efficient post-hoc explanation without repeated edge-level evaluations at deployment. Extensive experiments on multiple reasoning and coding benchmarks demonstrate that E2-Explainer identifies critical communication subgraphs that preserve successful collaboration. These subgraphs can also be executed directly to prune redundant communication edges, substantially reducing communication costs while maintaining competitive task performance.

</details>


### [274] [Reconcile Once, Write Anytime: A Trust-Tiered Librarian and a Multi-Agent Writer for Drift-Free, Point-in-Time Research](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12984)
*Xing Zhang,Yanwei Cui,Guanghui Wang,Peiyang He*

Main category: cs.MA

TL;DR: 本文提出一个两层的智能体系统：一个确定性的"图书馆管理员"将带时间戳的来源按信任层级组织成持续更新的知识库（含证据卡片、指标账本和声明图），一个可移植的"作者"运行时在任何知识截止时间T下生成无矛盾、有证据支撑的长篇研究报告。系统在包含6130个来源、555,926张证据卡片的公开语料上验证，消除了全部6845个跨部门矛盾，并在8个可复现实验中表现优异。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大语言模型生成的长篇研究报告存在漂移、自相矛盾、失去来源追溯等问题——同一指标出现不同数值，谣言与审计文件被同样自信地引用。现有RAG方法基于原始块进行逐查询检索，无法维持一个一致、时序正确的事实基础。

Method: 采用两层智能体架构：(1) 确定性"图书馆管理员"将带时间戳来源摄入信任分层的本体中，构建证据卡片、权威指标账本和声明图，形成持续更新的单一事实源；(2) 可移植多智能体"作者"运行时在知识截止时间T下只读取as_of<=T的证据（无前瞻），生成无矛盾、有证据的报告；红队评审意见回流至图书馆管理员实现自我修正。

Result: 在6130个来源（SEC EDGAR文件、BLS发布、维基百科）的语料上，从单一知识库生成4份不同主题的时点报告并运行8个可复现实验。共享指标账本将6845个跨部分矛盾降至零；分层优先选择在22/22金标准案例上正确（流行度优先基线仅9/22）；零媒体来源数字泄漏；红队反驳实现零人工编辑的自纠正；七种截止时间回放零前瞻违规；难度分层路由超过全Opus质量上限同时快3.7倍。

Conclusion: 通过将知识维护与报告编写彻底分离，并采用确定性证据分层机制，系统能够生成无矛盾、有可靠来源的长篇研究报告。该架构有效解决了LLM长报告中的漂移、矛盾与来源丢失问题，在质量和效率上均优于现有基线。

Abstract: Long-form research reports generated by large language models drift, contradict themselves, and lose provenance: the same metric appears with different values, and rumor is quoted as confidently as an audited filing. We present a two-tier agentic system that separates a maintained, point-in-time knowledge library from report writing. A deterministic "librarian" ingests timestamped sources into a trust-tiered ontology, layering evidence cards, an authoritative metric ledger, and a claim graph into an always-current source of truth, not per-query RAG over raw chunks. A portable multi-agent "writer" runtime then composes a contradiction-free, evidence-grounded report at any knowledge cutoff T, reading only evidence with as_of <= T (no look-ahead); red-team verdicts flow back into the librarian. We evaluate on a self-collected, public corpus of 6,130 sources yielding 555,926 evidence cards (SEC EDGAR filings across 295 issuers and 11 sectors, U.S. Bureau of Labor Statistics releases, and Wikipedia). From the one library we compose four point-in-time reports on distinct theses and run eight reproducible experiments, whose headline metrics come from a deterministic quality-control gate, itself validated by defect-injection meta-evaluation at recall 1.0 and precision 1.0. A shared metric ledger removes 6,845 cross-section contradictions to zero. Tier-first selection is correct on 22/22 gold cases where a popularity-first baseline scores only 9/22; trust tiering leaks zero media-sourced numbers, and no government statistic displaces a company's own filing. A red-team refutation propagates back and self-corrects a later run with zero manual edits. Replay exhibits zero look-ahead violations across seven cutoffs while the library grows from 235,373 to 555,312 cards. Difficulty-tiered model routing exceeds the all-Opus quality ceiling while running 3.7x faster than serial.

</details>


<div id='cs.SE'></div>

# cs.SE [[Back]](#toc)

### [275] [SynWeaver: Website-Prior Task and Trajectory Co-Synthesis for Web Agents](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12429)
*Ruitao Wang,Yuwen Hao,Menglin Yang*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文提出SynWeaver框架，通过结构化网站探索构建网站地图，利用网站先验知识生成更可靠的UI任务与轨迹数据，解决了现有探索式数据合成方法覆盖不全和任务幻觉的问题，在WebArena和WebVoyager上取得了优于现有基线的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Web智能体因缺乏网站特定监督而难以泛化到未见网站；现有探索式数据合成方法存在两个关键局限：(1) 无法覆盖网站的全部功能；(2) 缺乏网站先验知识导致生成幻觉任务，限制了轨迹合成的多样性和效率。

Method: SynWeaver包含三个步骤：(1) 结构化网站探索，构建覆盖多种功能不同页面状态和可执行交互的网站地图；(2) 从地图中提取页面级和转换级监督，训练具备网站先验的UI感知模型，生成更接地气的任务提议；(3) 协作式任务-轨迹联合合成，当任务与轨迹不一致时联合更新，并通过验证和修复生成可执行且语义对齐的监督数据。

Result: 在WebArena和WebVoyager上的实验表明，SynWeaver一致性地优于强合成基线，并能产生更有效的监督数据，提升智能体在域内和域外的泛化能力。

Conclusion: SynWeaver通过网站地图构建与UI感知模型的结合，有效解决了数据合成中的任务幻觉和覆盖不足问题，为Web智能体提供了更高质量的监督信号，验证了网站先验知识对下游任务-轨迹协同合成的重要性。

Abstract: Web agents often struggle to generalize to unseen websites because they lack website-specific supervision. Recent exploration-based data synthesis methods reduce manual annotation, but they still face two key limitations: they often fail to cover the full functionality of a website, and without sufficient website prior knowledge, they tend to propose hallucinated tasks, which in turn limits the diversity and efficiency of downstream trajectory synthesis. We present \textbf{SynWeaver}, a website-prior task-trajectory co-synthesis framework designed to address these challenges. SynWeaver first performs structured website exploration and constructs a website map that covers a broad set of functionally distinct page states and executable interactions on the target website. It then derives page-level and transition-level supervision from this map to train a UI-aware model with website-specific priors, enabling more grounded task proposals. Finally, SynWeaver performs collaborative task-trajectory synthesis, jointly updating the task and execution trajectory when they become inconsistent, and then verifies and repairs the collected results to produce executable, semantically aligned supervision. Experiments on WebArena and WebVoyager demonstrate that SynWeaver consistently outperforms strong synthesis baselines and yields more effective supervision for both in-domain and out-of-domain generalization.

</details>


### [276] [Specification-first convergence with an AI coding agent: a case study of dismantling a core architectural invariant across 189 files in a 717k-line codebase with no test oracle and no human code review](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12440)
*Joel Abenhaim*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文报告了一项大型架构重构案例研究：AI编码代理在规范优先协议下，无需人工审查代码且无预验证预言机，成功完成了一项被认为常规上必须重写才能完成的、涉及717,725行TypeScript生产代码库的核心生命周期不变量的拆除任务。代理通过31次审计轮次修正了201个缺陷，以两次连续零发现的验证通过为收敛标准，最终三天内完成，成本2,430美元，且未观察到任何bug。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索AI编码代理是否能够在无人审查、无预言机的条件下完成大规模架构重构——这类任务传统上被认为增量重构不可行，需要整体重写。作者希望通过规范优先协议来突破这一局限。

Method: 采用规范优先协议，包括五个阶段：(1) 代理生成形式化规范；(2) 14轮精化循环，审计规范与源代码一致性；(3) 原子化实施；(4) 编译/测试反馈循环；(5) 17轮验证循环，审计代码与冻结规范的符合性。收敛标准为连续两次验证轮次零发现。全程无人执行程序，所有证据（1,500+页法文规范与原始会话日志）公开发布。

Result: 代理成功完成重构：变更涉及189个文件（31个新增），两个提交总跨度288个文件、34,770行插入、16,422行删除。31次审计通过中修正了201个缺陷。跨首次会话和后续约30次会话，软件均按规范表现，未观察到任何bug。耗时三天，成本2,430美元。

Conclusion: 该案例表明，在规范的、可审计的协议约束下，AI编码代理可以完成传统上视为需要重写的大规模架构重构任务，且无需人工代码审查。独立的规范-审计框架（规范先于代码冻结，代码后于规范验证）可能提供了一种替代人工审查的可靠性保障机制。发布的完整证据促进了过程的透明性和第三方一致性检查的可能性。

Abstract: This paper reports a single, fully instrumented case study of a large-scale architectural refactoring by an AI coding agent under a specification-first protocol, with no human review of the generated code and no pre-existing oracle to validate the target behaviour. The task, dismantling a central invariant across a large interdependent codebase, was assessed by the author as effectively infeasible through incremental refactoring, the kind of change that conventionally calls for a rewrite instead. Under the protocol described here, the agent completed it successfully.
  The system is a 717,725-line production TypeScript application across 3,648 files. The task required dismantling a core lifetime invariant: the guarantee that a UI panel remains open for the duration of an AI request. The target behaviour was that a streaming generation survives the closing of its panel and can be reattached, on reopening, to the same live stream with no loss or duplication.
  The protocol: formal specification by the agent, 14 refinement cycles auditing that specification against the source code, atomic implementation, a compile/test feedback loop, then 17 verification cycles auditing the code against the frozen specification. Across 31 audit passes, 201 defects were corrected before any human executed the program. The convergence criterion was empirical: two consecutive verification passes returning zero findings.
  The change touched 189 files (31 new); with the extraction phase, the two commits total 288 files, 34,770 insertions, 16,422 deletions. Across the first and roughly thirty later sessions, the software behaved as specified, no bug observed. Elapsed: three days; cost: USD 2,430.
  The full specification and raw session logs, 1,500+ pages in French, are published as evidence, allowing inspection of the process and submission to a language model for consistency checking.

</details>


### [277] [Does It Render Everywhere? A Study of Cross-Environment Compatibility in MLLM-Generated Webpages](https://arxiv.org/abs/2608.12518)
*Ziyun Guo,Jingyu Xiao,Yuqiang Sun,Yintong Huo*

Main category: cs.SE

TL;DR: 本文首次系统性地研究了多模态大语言模型（MLLMs）生成网页的跨环境兼容性问题，构建了包含2,032个标注实例的WebCompat数据集（8个AI工具生成，9种浏览器和设备组合渲染），发现68%的生成网页存在至少一个兼容性问题，其中88.3%为破坏整体页面布局的严重故障。基于研究发现，作者开发了结合视觉截图与DOM树分析的轻量级兼容性问题检测器XCompat，在测试集上F1分数达到0.903，优于现有工具和LLM基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有MLLM网页生成评估仅局限于固定浏览器和设备配置下的视觉保真度评估，忽视了真实部署场景中跨环境渲染兼容性问题，缺乏系统性的实证研究。
